VULSENS & SECMOD APPLICATION – README
Version: v5 (2026-07-17)
Authors: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Repository: Internal model-testing workspace

Overview
The Vulsens & Secmod Toolkit is a modular Shiny application built for catastrophe modellers and property researchers. It provides a connected workspace to prepare, extract, analyse, and report on Vulnerability Sensitivity (Vulsens) and Secondary Modifier (Secmod) test cases across Moody’s RMS and Verisk modelling platforms.

The application is structured as a pipeline:

Input File Creation – generate standardised Location & Account files for vendor models.

Result Extraction – connect to Exposure and Result databases to retrieve loss metrics.

Secondary Modifier Analysis – run the full sensitivity workflow, customise charts, and build a gallery of results.

Cart & Reporting – collect plots and commentary, then export a polished PowerPoint, Word, or HTML report.

All modules share a unified visual language (navy/teal/amber) and a common cart system that persists per user.

Key Features
Multi‑vendor support: Moody’s RMS and Verisk (AIR)

Two test modules: Vulnerability Sensitivity and Secondary Modifiers

Interactive data validation and preview (DT tables)

Live customisation of every plot (colours, sizes, fonts, margins, labels)

One‑click Add to Cart – collect any plot with its current styling

Multi‑format reporting: PPTX, DOCX, and HTML (self‑contained)

Full R Markdown reporting for Secmod (customised HTML with all user overrides)

Persistent user carts (saved to disk)

Responsive two‑column layout with independent scrollbars and progress stepper

Technology Stack
R (4.0+) with Shiny, shinydashboard, shinyjs

Data handling: data.table, dplyr, RODBC (SQL Server)

Reporting: officer (PPTX/DOCX), rmarkdown + knitr (HTML), ggplot2

Styling: custom CSS with Font Awesome icons

Deployment: runs locally or on a Shiny Server / RStudio Connect

Directory Structure (key folders)
text
├── R/                 # Core utility functions and report generators
├── server/            # Module server logic (one per app module)
├── ui/                # Module UI definitions
├── www/               # Static assets (CSS, JS, images, template PPTX)
├── data/              # Sample input files for Secmod (optional)
└── cart/              # User cart persistence (auto‑created)
File‑by‑File Descriptions
Below is a detailed “about me” for every .R and .Rmd file in the application, organised by folder.

Root Folder
Code name: Combined_Code_Final.R
Location: ./
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

Provides a function combine_code_with_prompt() that scans a folder for code files of selected types, compiles them into a single text file with a header and table of contents.

Includes write_folder_architecture() to generate a tree‑view summary of a project folder, showing file sizes and types.

Contains preset functions (combine_r_files(), combine_all_code()) for quick usage.

The script is used to generate MetaApp_combined_code.txt and MetaApp_architecture.txt for archival and documentation purposes.

Code name: MetaApp_Final.R
Location: ./
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

Serves as the entry point for the Shiny application.

Sources all utility, server, and UI modules.

Defines the ui using dashboardPage (header, sidebar, body) with a unified brand header containing a live cart badge.

Defines the server logic, including the reactive cart, page navigation via sidebar or header buttons, and module calls (callModule).

Ties all modules together into a single running app via shinyApp(ui, server).

R/ Folder
Code name: cart_utils.R
Location: R/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

Defines functions to manage user‑specific carts stored as .rds files in a cart/ directory.

get_cart_path() builds the file path; load_cart() and save_cart() read/write the cart list.

generate_item_id() creates a unique identifier for each cart item (timestamp + random suffix).

Used by cart_server, secmod_server, and other modules that interact with the cart.

Code name: docx_generator.R
Location: R/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

Builds a scientific Word (.docx) report from a cart .rds file.

Creates a title page with logo, a Table of Contents (using native Word fields), and one numbered section per cart item.

Embeds each plot as a captioned figure and renders commentary as an indented block‑quote.

Adds a live page‑number footer (Page X of Y) and forces field updates on open.

Uses the officer package and strips default heading numbering to avoid duplication.

Code name: function_Secmod.R
Location: R/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

Contains the core analytical functions for the Secondary Modifier workflow.

finaltable() and finaltable_allUSA() transform AAL data by classification and modifier.

indmod() generates individual modifier plots (one per modifier) across states.

STATEminmax() and Countryminmax() compute credit/penalty ranges at state and country levels.

Credit_Penalty() creates the combined bar chart showing modifiers with >10% sensitivity change.

STATE_plot() produces state‑specific plots for SFD vs COM, with helpers to save all plots as PNG.

Code name: input_preparation_helpers.R
Location: R/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

Provides helper functions for the Input File Creation module.

Manages peril lookups, vendor‑specific metadata (Moody’s vs Verisk).

Reads uploaded or sample location/combination files and validates required columns.

Builds output file names and expands secondary modifier columns.

Contains separate builders for Moody’s/Verisk and for Vulsens/Secmod, producing .csv Location and Account files.

Code name: ppt_generator.R
Location: R/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

Generates a PowerPoint (.pptx) report from a cart .rds file.

Uses a widescreen (16:9) template with a navy/light‑blue theme.

Creates a title slide, index (contents) slides (chunked for long carts), and one slide per plot and per commentary block.

Features a progress dot‑map (Beamer‑style) in the footer and a slim header with an item counter.

Applies the app’s unified colour palette and uses officer for all slide composition.

Code name: result_extraction_helpers.R
Location: R/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

Supplies helper functions for the Result Extraction module.

Handles SQL Server connections (both trusted and credential‑based for RMS).

Queries ExposureSet and ResultSet metadata and extracts location and AAL data.

Supports both RMS and Verisk database schemas.

Merges extracted losses with input templates, computes medians by state/county/region, and produces downloadable CSV outputs.

Code name: rmd_generator.R
Location: R/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

Knits the vulsen_report_template.Rmd into a self‑contained HTML report from a cart .rds file.

Resolves all paths to absolute and sets knit_root_dir to the app’s working directory to avoid relative‑path issues.

Passes the cart data, username, report title, and logo as parameters to the template.

Disables toc_float so the TOC is statically rendered (no JavaScript required) and sets the browser tab title via pandoc_args.

Code name: secmod_customized_template.Rmd
Location: R/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

An R Markdown template for the customized Secondary Modifier report (stage 6 of the Secmod UI).

Accepts params containing pre‑styled plot bundles (credit_penalty, state_lob_plots, modifier_plots) and an optional table.

Renders each bundle by saving the ggplot to a PNG at the user‑specified dimensions/DPI and embedding it as an <img> with inline CSS.

Lays out the report with sections for country‑wide impact, state‑wise plots, and individual modifier plots.

Used when the user clicks “Generate customized report” in the Secmod module.

Code name: vulsen_report_template.Rmd
Location: R/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

An R Markdown template for the general Vulsens HTML report (from the Cart page).

Builds a masthead with logo, title, and byline; then iterates over cart items to display each plot and commentary.

Styles the report with a left‑hand TOC sidebar (static, no JS) using custom CSS.

Embeds images as base64 URIs for portability.

Used by rmd_generator.R when the user selects HTML export from the Cart.

server/ Folder
Code name: cart_server.R
Location: server/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

Implements the server logic for the Cart module.

Renders the list of cart items with preview thumbnails, timestamps, and remove buttons.

Handles removal of individual items and “Remove All” with a confirmation modal.

Manages report generation: when the user clicks “Generate Report”, it triggers generate_cart_ppt, generate_cart_docx, or generate_cart_html depending on the selected format.

Serves the generated file via a custom JavaScript download trigger (cart-trigger-download).

Code name: home_server.R
Location: server/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

Provides the server logic for the Home page.

Observes clicks on hero buttons, workflow pipeline steps, and feature cards, and sets a navigation reactive value.

Renders the live cart count in the hero stats and the cart button badge.

Returns the navigation reactive value for the top‑level server to switch the active page.

Code name: input_preparation_server.R
Location: server/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

Implements server logic for the Input File Creation module.

Manages sample/uploaded data switching, dynamic UI for version and subperil, and previews of location/combination files.

On “Create Input Files”, calls build_files() (from input_preparation_helpers.R) and stores the generated data frames.

Provides download handlers for the generated Location and Account CSV files.

Renders a progress stepper in the right panel to show completion status.

Code name: result_extraction_server.R
Location: server/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

Implements server logic for the Result Extraction module.

Manages SQL Server connections (including RMS credential popups), database listing, and loading of ExposureSet/ResultSet tables.

Handles sample file switching and dynamic version panels (1 or 2 versions for Vulsens).

On “Create Result Files”, runs run_extraction() and stores the resulting data frames.

Provides download handlers for individual CSV outputs and a ZIP of all files.

Updates a progress stepper to reflect the current stage.

Code name: secmod_server.R
Location: server/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

The largest server module, handling all logic for the Secondary Modifier section.

Manages data upload (State AAL, Country AAL, mapping file) and calls finaltable, STATEminmax, etc.

Generates credit/penalty plots, state‑wise plots, and modifier plots, storing them in reactive values.

Implements per‑plot override controls (size, colours, text, legend) using apply_plot_overrides().

Provides “Add to Cart” for individual plots and “Add all” for galleries, with toast notifications.

Generates the customized HTML report via rmarkdown::render() using the secmod_customized_template.Rmd and passes the current plot bundles.

Includes demo data loading and report generation locks (preventing concurrent runs).

ui/ Folder
Code name: cart_ui.R
Location: ui/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

Defines the UI for the Cart module.

Displays a summary of saved items and buttons for “Remove All” and “Generate Report”.

Includes a selectInput for export format (PPTX/DOCX/HTML).

Contains a placeholder uiOutput for the cart items list, which is rendered server‑side.

Includes JavaScript to handle the file download trigger.

Code name: home_ui.R
Location: ui/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

Defines the UI for the Home page.

Builds a hero section with a gradient background, stat counters, and primary call‑to‑action buttons.

Creates four feature cards (Input File Creation, Result Extraction, Secondary Modifier, Cart & Reports) with icons and descriptions.

Each card and button has a click handler that sets a Shiny input to navigate to the corresponding module.

Code name: input_preparation_ui.R
Location: ui/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

Defines the UI for the Input File Creation module.

Implements a two‑column layout: left panel with model selection, exposure settings, file uploads, and action buttons; right panel with status, info, progress stepper, and tabbed previews.

Uses sec_hint() for explanatory tooltips.

Provides download buttons for sample files and generated outputs.

Code name: result_extraction_ui.R
Location: ui/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

Defines the UI for the Result Extraction module.

Similarly two‑column: left panel for model selection, uploads, version panels with connection/load buttons; right panel for status, info, progress stepper, and tabbed views for ExposureSet, ResultSet, input preview, and output preview.

Dynamically renders version panels (1 or 2) and database selection dropdowns.

Code name: secmod_ui.R
Location: ui/
Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
Date: 2026
What this code does:

Defines the UI for the Secondary Modifier module.

Two‑column layout: left panel contains six stage cards (Settings, Inputs, Credit/Penalty, State Sensitivity, Individual Modifier, Report).

Right panel shows the progress stepper, an info panel describing the workflow, and a tabset with the Min/Max Table, Credit/Penalty plot, State Sensitivity gallery, and Individual Modifier gallery.

Each gallery includes a collapsible “Gallery defaults” control bar and per‑plot override panels with extensive customisation options (via sec_plot_card_gallery()).

The Report stage has a “Generate Report” button and a “Download Report” button (state‑locked until generation completes).

Includes JavaScript to scroll to sections and toggle gallery panels.

================================================================================
ABOUT THIS APP — VulSen Analytics (Vulnerability Sensitivity & Secondary
Modifier Shiny Application)
================================================================================


################################################################################
# WHOLE APP README
################################################################################

# App Name: VulSen Analytics (Vulnerability Sensitivity & Secondary Modifier App)
# Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
# Date:
# What this app does:
#    1. Gives catastrophe-modeling analysts a single Shiny dashboard covering
#       the full workflow for Vulnerability Sensitivity and Secondary Modifier
#       (Secmod) analysis: preparing vendor input files, extracting AAL/loss
#       results from RMS or Verisk databases, running the Secmod calculations
#       and generating the underlying plots, and packaging chosen plots +
#       commentary into a shareable report.
#    2. "Input File Creation" module builds vendor-ready location and
#       combination files (Moody's/RMS or Verisk format) for a chosen peril,
#       sub-peril, and country, either from sample data or user uploads.
#    3. "Result Extraction" module connects to RMS or Verisk SQL databases
#       (via RODBC), pulls ExposureSet/ResultSet AAL data for one or two
#       model versions, and writes out the comparison result files analysts
#       need for the vulnerability/Secmod work.
#    4. "Secondary Modifier" module is the analytical core: it ingests the
#       extracted AAL data, computes relative loss-cost sensitivity by
#       modifier/state/LOB, and renders a large gallery of styled ggplot
#       charts (state maps, credit/penalty bar charts, individual modifier
#       plots) with live user-adjustable colours, sizes, and labels.
#    5. "Cart" lets users collect selected plots and written commentary from
#       any module across a session, then export the whole collection as a
#       branded PowerPoint (.pptx), Word (.docx), or self-contained HTML
#       report in one click.
#
# How the pieces fit together:
#    - MetaApp_Final.R is the single entry point: it loads packages, sources
#      every module below, assembles the shinydashboard UI/server, and wires
#      up navigation between Home / Input Prep / Result Extraction / Secmod /
#      Cart.
#    - R/ holds shared, non-Shiny logic: data-crunching + plotting functions
#      (function_Secmod.R), helper functions for the other two modules
#      (input_preparation_helpers.R, result_extraction_helpers.R), the cart's
#      persistence layer (cart_utils.R), and the three report generators
#      (ppt_generator.R, docx_generator.R, rmd_generator.R) plus their Rmd
#      templates (vulsen_report_template.Rmd, secmod_customized_template.Rmd).
#    - server/ and ui/ hold one Shiny module (server + UI pair) per app page:
#      home, input_preparation, result_extraction, secmod, cart.
#    - www/ holds static assets: the Gallagher Re / VulSen logos, the three
#      stylesheets (dashboard_style.css, Meta_styles.css, secmod_style.css),
#      a small JS toast-notification helper, and the widescreen PowerPoint
#      template used by ppt_generator.R.
#    - data/secmod_sample/ ships sample AAL and modifier-name CSVs so every
#      module works out of the box before any real vendor data is connected.
#
# Report formats produced (same content, three renderers):
#    - PPTX  -> R/ppt_generator.R  (uses www/widescreen_template.pptx)
#    - DOCX  -> R/docx_generator.R (uses officer's built-in Word template)
#    - HTML  -> R/rmd_generator.R  (knits R/vulsen_report_template.Rmd)
#    Secmod also has its own dedicated, user-customized HTML report path via
#    R/secmod_customized_template.Rmd, triggered directly from
#    server/secmod_server.R (separate from the Cart export flow above).
#
# Key third-party packages relied on across the app: shiny, shinydashboard,
# shinyjs, data.table, dplyr, tidyr, forcats, stringr, DT, RODBC, glue,
# ggplot2, colourpicker, officer, rmarkdown, base64enc.


################################################################################
# INDIVIDUAL CODE FILES
################################################################################

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Root
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Code name: MetaApp_Final.R
# Code Location: root/
# Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
# Date:
# What this code does:
#    1. Acts as the single entry point of the Shiny app: loads all required
#       packages (shiny, shinydashboard, shinyjs, data.table, dplyr, DT,
#       RODBC, glue, stringr, ggplot2, colourpicker) and sources every R,
#       server, and ui module the app needs.
#    2. Builds the dashboardPage UI shell: a unified header (logo + brand
#       name + live cart-count badge), a collapsible sidebar with Home,
#       Input File Creation, Result Extraction, and Secondary Modifier menu
#       items, and a body that links the app's three CSS files and toast JS.
#    3. Defines the top-level server: tracks a session-only cart
#       (reactiveVal), the currently active page, wires up navigation from
#       both the sidebar and the header cart button, calls each module's
#       server function (home, input prep, result extraction, secmod, cart),
#       and switches which module's UI is rendered based on the active page.

# Code name: Combined_Code_Final.R
# Code Location: root/
# Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
# Date:
# What this code does:
#    1. Developer/documentation utility (not part of the running app) that
#       walks a project folder and combines every source file of chosen
#       types (R, Rmd, css, js, html, py, txt, json, yml, sh, md, config,
#       xml) into one big annotated text file, with a table of contents and
#       a per-file header (name/path/type/size/lines) -- this is exactly the
#       tool that produced MetaApp_combined_code.txt.
#    2. write_folder_architecture() scans the same folder and writes a
#       human-readable directory-tree report with per-file sizes and a
#       summary by file type and largest files -- this produced
#       MetaApp_architecture.txt.
#    3. Provides convenience wrappers (combine_r_files, combine_all_code,
#       quick_combine) and a set_script_wd() helper that auto-detects and
#       sets the working directory to wherever the script itself lives,
#       whether run interactively in RStudio or via Rscript.

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------
# R/  (shared logic, plotting, and report generators)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Code name: cart_utils.R
# Code Location: R/
# Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
# Date:
# What this code does:
#    1. Defines the on-disk cart storage layout: each user's cart is saved
#       as cart/<sanitized_username>_cart.rds, a list of items shaped like
#       list(id, module, plot, commentary, timestamp).
#    2. get_cart_path()/load_cart()/save_cart() create the cart directory if
#       needed, sanitize the username into a safe filename, and read/write
#       the .rds file (returning an empty list if none exists yet or the
#       file can't be read).
#    3. generate_item_id() builds a unique id for each new cart entry from a
#       millisecond timestamp plus a random 4-digit suffix, so items added
#       in quick succession never collide.

# Code name: docx_generator.R
# Code Location: R/
# Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
# Date:
# What this code does:
#    1. Builds a Word (.docx) report from a saved cart .rds file -- the
#       "paper" counterpart to ppt_generator.R -- using the officer package
#       and the app's shared navy/light-blue colour palette, so no separate
#       .dotx template is needed.
#    2. Lays out a title page (natural-shape logo, report title, byline,
#       thin accent rule) followed by an auto-updating native Word Table of
#       Contents, then one numbered "Heading 1" section per cart item with
#       a captioned figure ("Figure N. <Module>") and an indented,
#       italicised block-quote for the commentary.
#    3. Includes fixes to strip duplicate Word auto-numbering on headings
#       and to force updateFields so the TOC page numbers are correct the
#       moment the document is opened, plus helper functions to fit an
#       image into a box while preserving its aspect ratio and to draw a
#       coloured horizontal rule.

# Code name: function_Secmod.R
# Code Location: R/
# Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
# Date:
# What this code does:
#    1. Holds the core, non-Shiny data-crunching and plotting logic for the
#       Secondary Modifier module: finaltable() reshapes raw AAL data by
#       splitting the Classification column into type/modifier, matching
#       modifier codes to human-readable names, and preparing it for
#       plotting.
#    2. indmod()/indmod_forpng() build the per-modifier relative-loss-cost
#       bar charts (faceted by state, coloured by SFD/COM type), padding in
#       placeholder rows so every state facet renders even with missing
#       data, and can save each modifier's chart to PNG.
#    3. Credit_Penalty() builds the credit/penalty sensitivity bar chart
#       from the country-wide min/max table; STATE_plot()/save_STATE_plots()
#       build and export the individual state-level modifier plots used
#       throughout the Secmod UI's plot gallery.

# Code name: input_preparation_helpers.R
# Code Location: R/
# Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
# Date:
# What this code does:
#    1. Supplies the lookup and metadata tables the "Input File Creation"
#       module needs: peril_lookup() (peril -> sub-peril list) and
#       vendor_meta() (Moody's/RMS vs Verisk peril codes, policy types, and
#       abbreviations).
#    2. Reads either a user-uploaded file or a bundled sample
#       (read_uploaded_or_sample()), validates required columns are present
#       (validate_required_cols()), and builds standardised output file
#       names (build_output_names()).
#    3. build_moodys_vulnerability()/build_moodys_secmod()/
#       build_verisk_vulnerability()/build_verisk_secmod() assemble the
#       actual vendor-formatted location/combination files for each
#       vendor + module combination; build_files() dispatches to the right
#       one based on the user's vendor/module selection and writes the
#       results out.

# Code name: ppt_generator.R
# Code Location: R/
# Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
# Date:
# What this code does:
#    1. Builds a branded, widescreen PowerPoint (.pptx) deck from a saved
#       cart .rds file using officer and the shared VulSen colour theme
#       (navy header band, light-blue body, accent underline) on top of
#       www/widescreen_template.pptx.
#    2. Produces: a title/branding slide with the Gallagher Re logo, one or
#       more chunked "Contents" (index) slides listing every cart item with
#       its timestamp, and then one plot slide plus one commentary slide per
#       cart item, each carrying a consistent header/footer, a "Title |
#       01/04" item counter, and a small dot-map showing progress through
#       the deck.
#    3. Provides supporting internal helpers to flatten a colour into a PNG
#       background/header band, auto-crop transparent padding from the
#       logo PNG so it isn't scaled down to a speck, fit an image into a
#       target box preserving aspect ratio, and draw the section progress
#       dot-map, page footer, and item counter badge used on every slide.

# Code name: result_extraction_helpers.R
# Code Location: R/
# Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
# Date:
# What this code does:
#    1. Supplies vendor/module lookup helpers for the "Result Extraction"
#       module (vendor_tag, module_tag, default_server, default_version_
#       label, sample_paths_extract) and shares the peril_lookup() table
#       used elsewhere in the app.
#    2. Wraps RODBC database access: connect_sql() opens a connection for
#       RMS or Verisk, list_databases()/get_exposure_set()/get_result_set()
#       list and validate available databases, and safe_sql() runs a query
#       with consistent error handling.
#    3. loc_rms()/aal_rms()/loc_verisk()/aal_verisk() pull location and AAL
#       result data for each vendor's schema; validate_input_file(),
#       load_region_file(), attach_template(), build_identifier(), and
#       summarise_by() clean and reshape that data; make_outputs(),
#       output_filenames(), and run_extraction() tie the whole pipeline
#       together and write the final comparison result files.

# Code name: rmd_generator.R
# Code Location: R/
# Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
# Date:
# What this code does:
#    1. Builds a self-contained, brandable HTML report from a saved cart
#       .rds file -- the "web" counterpart to ppt_generator.R and
#       docx_generator.R -- by knitting R/vulsen_report_template.Rmd via
#       rmarkdown::render(), using the same navy/light-blue VulSen theme.
#    2. Resolves all file paths (cart, logo, output) to absolute paths and
#       pins knit_root_dir to the Shiny app's own working directory before
#       rendering, since rmarkdown otherwise knits from the template's own
#       folder and breaks relative paths passed in as params.
#    3. Documents and fixes two Pandoc rendering quirks: disabling YAML
#       title/author/date so Pandoc doesn't inject an automatic header
#       block above the hand-built logo/title masthead, and turning off
#       toc_float so Pandoc bakes a fully populated, JavaScript-free table
#       of contents into the page instead of one that renders empty in
#       sanitized viewers.

# Code name: secmod_customized_template.Rmd
# Code Location: R/
# Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
# Date:
# What this code does:
#    1. R Markdown template for the Secondary Modifier module's own
#       "customized report" export (rendered directly from
#       server/secmod_server.R, separate from the Cart's ppt/docx/html
#       export path) -- an HTML report with a floating table of contents,
#       numbered sections, and code folding.
#    2. Accepts pre-built, already-styled ggplot objects via params
#       (credit_penalty, state_lob_plots, modifier_plots) reflecting the
#       user's live colour/size/label overrides at the moment "Generate
#       customized report" was clicked, plus an optional country-wide
#       min/max table, and simply rasterizes and lays each one out at its
#       own chosen width/height/dpi.
#    3. Deliberately does no recomputation, theme_set(), or fallback
#       styling of its own -- if a required plot bundle is missing or
#       malformed, the template stops rather than silently substituting a
#       default, since every plot is expected to already be final.

# Code name: vulsen_report_template.Rmd
# Code Location: R/
# Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
# Date:
# What this code does:
#    1. R Markdown template knitted by rmd_generator.R to produce the
#       Cart's HTML export -- a single-page, self-contained report styled
#       with the VulSen navy/accent theme, custom Google fonts, and a
#       hand-built masthead (logo, then title, then byline/date) so the
#       logo always leads the page.
#    2. Loops over every item in the supplied cart .rds file and renders
#       each one's plot and commentary in the same structure used by the
#       pptx/docx exports, giving all three report formats the same content
#       and reading order.
#    3. Bakes a static, JavaScript-free left-hand table of contents at
#       compile time (toc_float off) and styles that same markup with pure
#       CSS (position: sticky, rounded card, hover state, mobile
#       breakpoint) so the TOC still looks and behaves like a floating
#       sidebar even in viewers that block scripts.

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------
# server/  (one Shiny server module per app page)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Code name: cart_server.R
# Code Location: server/
# Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
# Date:
# What this code does:
#    1. Implements the server logic behind the Cart page: renders each
#       saved item's plot (via a base64 data-URI thumbnail generated by
#       plot_to_data_uri()) and commentary, and lets the user remove items
#       or clear the whole cart, persisting changes through cart_utils.R.
#    2. run_report_generation() drives the "Export" action: depending on
#       the user's chosen format, it calls generate_cart_ppt(),
#       generate_cart_docx(), or generate_cart_html() and writes the result
#       to a dedicated temp download directory that's registered as a
#       Shiny resource path (addResourcePath) so the browser can fetch it.
#    3. Triggers the actual file download via a JS-initiated link click
#       (rather than a standard downloadHandler), and reports progress/
#       errors and a friendly format_label() (e.g. "PowerPoint (.pptx)")
#       back to the UI while generation runs.

# Code name: home_server.R
# Code Location: server/
# Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
# Date:
# What this code does:
#    1. Implements the server logic for the Home/landing page module.
#    2. Listens for clicks on the hero buttons and workflow-pipeline step
#       cards and translates each one into a navigation target via a
#       reactiveVal, which the top-level app server (MetaApp_Final.R)
#       observes to switch the active page (e.g. to Input Prep or Cart).
#    3. Surfaces the current live cart count on the home page so users can
#       see at a glance how many items they've already saved.

# Code name: input_preparation_server.R
# Code Location: server/
# Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
# Date:
# What this code does:
#    1. Implements the server logic for the "Input File Creation" page:
#       defaults to bundled sample location/combination files, but switches
#       to the user's own uploads as soon as they provide one.
#    2. Reacts to the user's vendor/module/peril/country/sub-peril
#       selections, previews the loaded location and combination data in
#       tables, and calls build_files() (from
#       input_preparation_helpers.R) to generate the vendor-formatted
#       output files on demand.
#    3. Exposes the generated files back to the UI for preview and
#       download, keeping track of generation state via a reactiveVal so
#       the download controls only activate once files actually exist.

# Code name: result_extraction_server.R
# Code Location: server/
# Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
# Date:
# What this code does:
#    1. Implements the server logic for the "Result Extraction" page:
#       manages one or two RODBC SQL connections (RMS or Verisk) at a time,
#       tracking connection objects, available databases, and selected
#       ExposureSet/ResultSet per version via reactiveValues.
#    2. connect_version() handles connecting to a chosen server/database
#       for either model version, using default server/version-label
#       helpers from result_extraction_helpers.R as sensible starting
#       points, and updates the app status message as each step succeeds
#       or fails.
#    3. Runs the extraction pipeline (run_extraction()) once inputs are
#       validated, previews the resulting comparison tables, and packages
#       the output files into a downloadable zip via write_zip().

# Code name: secmod_server.R
# Code Location: server/
# Author: Gallagher Re, Property Research
# Date:
# What this code does:
#    1. Implements the server logic for the Secondary Modifier module (the
#       largest module in the app): loads and cleans the extracted AAL
#       data, applies the default/known modifier-name lookup, and calls
#       into function_Secmod.R to compute relative loss-cost sensitivity
#       and build the full gallery of state/modifier/credit-penalty plots.
#    2. Manages a six-section, two-column UI workflow with live status
#       badges (Done/Pending) per section, and lets users override each
#       plot's colours, size, DPI, and labels through colourpicker/shinyjs
#       controls before finalising it (apply_plot_overrides()).
#    3. Adds finished plots and written commentary to the shared cart
#       (via cart_utils.R) for later export, and separately renders its own
#       dedicated "customized" HTML report by knitting
#       R/secmod_customized_template.Rmd directly with the user's final,
#       styled plot bundles.

set_script_wd <- function() {
  # Try RStudio (if running in RStudio)
  if (requireNamespace("rstudioapi", quietly = TRUE) && rstudioapi::isAvailable()) {
    script_path <- rstudioapi::getActiveDocumentContext()$path
    if (nzchar(script_path)) {
      setwd(dirname(script_path))
      message("Working directory set to: ", getwd())
      return(invisible(getwd()))
    }
  }
  
  # Try command-line arguments (Rscript)
  args <- commandArgs(trailingOnly = FALSE)
  file_arg <- grep("^--file=", args, value = TRUE)
  if (length(file_arg) > 0) {
    script_path <- sub("^--file=", "", file_arg[1])
    # If path is relative, convert to absolute
    if (!file.exists(script_path)) {
      # Try to find absolute path by joining with current dir
      script_path <- file.path(getwd(), script_path)
    }
    if (file.exists(script_path)) {
      setwd(dirname(script_path))
      message("Working directory set to: ", getwd())
      return(invisible(getwd()))
    }
  }
  
  # Try using sys.calls() to find source file
  calls <- sys.calls()
  for (i in rev(seq_along(calls))) {
    call <- as.character(calls[[i]])[1]
    if (grepl("^source\\(", call)) {
      # Extract filename
      script_path <- gsub("^source\\(", "", call)
      script_path <- gsub("\\)$", "", script_path)
      script_path <- tryCatch(eval(parse(text = script_path)), error = function(e) NULL)
      if (is.character(script_path) && file.exists(script_path)) {
        setwd(dirname(script_path))
        message("Working directory set to: ", getwd())
        return(invisible(getwd()))
      }
    }
  }
  
  # Fallback: use current working directory
  message("Could not detect script directory. Keeping current working directory: ", getwd())
  invisible(getwd())
}

# Call the function
set_script_wd()




# Enhanced modular function to combine code files
combine_code_with_prompt <- function(parent_folder = getwd(),
                                     output_file = NULL,
                                     file_types = NULL,
                                     recursive = TRUE,
                                     exclude_patterns = c("\\.rds$", "\\.RData$", "\\.Rhistory$", 
                                                          "~$", "\\.bak$", "\\.log$"),
                                     max_file_size_mb = 10,
                                     include_header = TRUE,
                                     include_structure = TRUE,
                                     quiet = FALSE) {
  
  # Check if folder exists
  if (!dir.exists(parent_folder)) {
    stop("❌ Folder not found: ", parent_folder)
  }
  
  # Normalize path
  parent_folder <- normalizePath(parent_folder, winslash = "/")
  
  # Set default output file name if not provided
  if (is.null(output_file)) {
    output_file <- file.path(parent_folder, paste0("Masanta_Dental_combined_code_", 
                                                   format(Sys.time(), "%Y%m%d_%H%M%S"), 
                                                   ".txt"))
  }
  
  # Define available file types
  available_types <- list(
    "R Scripts" = "r",
    "R Scripts (uppercase)" = "R", 
    "R Markdown" = "Rmd",
    "CSS Styles" = "css",
    "JavaScript" = "js",
    "HTML" = "html",
    "Python" = "py",
    "Text files" = "txt",
    "JSON" = "json",
    "YAML" = "yml",
    "Shell Scripts" = "sh",
    "Markdown" = "md",
    "Configuration" = c("conf", "config", "ini"),
    "XML" = "xml"
  )
  
  # If file_types not provided interactively, show menu
  if (is.null(file_types)) {
    cat("\n", paste(rep("=", 80), collapse = ""), "\n")
    cat("📁 SELECT FILE TYPES TO COMBINE\n")
    cat(paste(rep("=", 80), collapse = ""), "\n\n")
    
    # Display menu with categories
    type_names <- names(available_types)
    for (i in seq_along(type_names)) {
      ext_display <- paste0(".", paste(available_types[[i]], collapse = ", ."))
      cat(sprintf("%2d. %-25s (%s)\n", i, type_names[i], ext_display))
    }
    cat("\n 0. Select all types\n")
    
    # Get user selection
    cat("\nEnter numbers separated by commas (e.g., 1,2,3): ")
    selection <- readline(prompt = "")
    
    # Parse selection
    if (trimws(selection) == "0" || tolower(selection) == "all") {
      selected_extensions <- unique(unlist(available_types))
      cat("\n✅ Selected all file types\n")
    } else {
      selected_nums <- as.numeric(unlist(strsplit(selection, ",")))
      selected_nums <- selected_nums[!is.na(selected_nums) & selected_nums >= 1 & selected_nums <= length(available_types)]
      if (length(selected_nums) == 0) {
        cat("❌ Invalid selection. Using all file types.\n")
        selected_extensions <- unique(unlist(available_types))
      } else {
        selected_extensions <- unique(unlist(available_types[selected_nums]))
        cat("\n✅ Selected extensions:", paste(selected_extensions, collapse = ", "), "\n")
      }
    }
  } else {
    selected_extensions <- file_types
    cat("\n✅ Using predefined extensions:", paste(selected_extensions, collapse = ", "), "\n")
  }
  
  # Build pattern
  pattern <- paste0("\\.(", paste(selected_extensions, collapse = "|"), ")$")
  
  # Find all matching files
  if (!quiet) cat("\n🔍 Searching for files...\n")
  all_files <- list.files(
    path = parent_folder,
    pattern = pattern,
    full.names = TRUE,
    recursive = recursive,
    ignore.case = TRUE
  )
  
  # Remove duplicates and apply exclude patterns
  all_files <- unique(all_files)
  
  # Filter by exclude patterns
  exclude_pattern <- paste(exclude_patterns, collapse = "|")
  if (exclude_pattern != "") {
    all_files <- all_files[!grepl(exclude_pattern, basename(all_files), ignore.case = TRUE)]
  }
  
  # Filter by file size
  file_sizes <- file.info(all_files)$size / (1024^2)  # Size in MB
  all_files <- all_files[file_sizes <= max_file_size_mb | is.na(file_sizes)]
  
  if (length(all_files) == 0) {
    cat("❌ No files found with selected extensions.\n")
    return(invisible(NULL))
  }
  
  if (!quiet) cat("✅ Found", length(all_files), "files\n")
  
  # Sort files by directory for better organization
  all_files <- sort(all_files)
  
  # Open connection for output
  con <- file(output_file, "w", encoding = "UTF-8")
  
  # ============================================================
  # WRITE HEADER
  # ============================================================
  if (include_header) {
    writeLines(paste0(rep("#", 80), collapse = ""), con)
    writeLines(paste0("#", paste(rep(" ", 78), collapse = ""), "#"), con)
    writeLines("#                     APPLICATION - COMPLETE CODE                      #", con)
    writeLines(paste0("#", paste(rep(" ", 78), collapse = ""), "#"), con)
    writeLines(paste0(rep("#", 80), collapse = ""), con)
    writeLines(paste("# Generated:", format(Sys.time(), "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")), con)
    writeLines(paste("# Source Folder:", parent_folder), con)
    writeLines(paste("# Total Files:", length(all_files)), con)
    writeLines(paste("# File Types:", paste(selected_extensions, collapse = ", ")), con)
    writeLines(paste("# Recursive:", recursive), con)
    writeLines(paste("# Max File Size:", max_file_size_mb, "MB"), con)
    writeLines(paste0(rep("#", 80), collapse = ""), con)
    writeLines("", con)
    writeLines("", con)
  }
  
  # ============================================================
  # SECTION 1: TABLE OF CONTENTS
  # ============================================================
  if (include_structure) {
    writeLines(paste0(rep("#", 80), collapse = ""), con)
    writeLines("#                          📑 TABLE OF CONTENTS                                 #", con)
    writeLines(paste0(rep("#", 80), collapse = ""), con)
    writeLines("", con)
    
    # Group files by directory
    files_by_dir <- split(all_files, dirname(all_files))
    
    toc_counter <- 1
    for (dir in names(files_by_dir)) {
      rel_path <- gsub(paste0("^", parent_folder, "/?"), "", dir)
      if (rel_path == "") rel_path <- "Root"
      
      writeLines(sprintf("%3d. 📁 %s/ (%d files)", toc_counter, rel_path, length(files_by_dir[[dir]])), con)
      toc_counter <- toc_counter + 1
      
      for (file in files_by_dir[[dir]]) {
        file_name <- basename(file)
        writeLines(sprintf("       📄 %s", file_name), con)
      }
      writeLines("", con)
    }
    writeLines("", con)
  }
  
  # ============================================================
  # SECTION 2: FOLDER STRUCTURE
  # ============================================================
  if (include_structure) {
    writeLines(paste0(rep("#", 80), collapse = ""), con)
    writeLines("#                          📁 FOLDER STRUCTURE                                 #", con)
    writeLines(paste0(rep("#", 80), collapse = ""), con)
    writeLines("", con)
    
    # Get all unique directories
    all_dirs <- sort(unique(dirname(all_files)))
    
    for (dir in all_dirs) {
      # Calculate relative path
      rel_path <- gsub(paste0("^", parent_folder, "/?"), "", dir)
      if (rel_path == "") rel_path <- "📁 Root"
      
      # Count files in this directory
      files_in_dir <- all_files[dirname(all_files) == dir]
      
      writeLines(paste("\n📂", rel_path, paste0("(", length(files_in_dir), " files)")), con)
      writeLines(paste(rep("─", 80), collapse = ""), con)
      
      # List files in this directory with metadata
      for (file in sort(files_in_dir)) {
        file_name <- basename(file)
        file_ext <- tools::file_ext(file_name)
        file_size_mb <- file.info(file)$size / (1024^2)
        size_str <- ifelse(file_size_mb < 0.001, 
                           paste0(round(file_size_mb * 1024, 1), " KB"),
                           paste0(round(file_size_mb, 2), " MB"))
        
        # Add icon based on file type
        icon <- switch(tolower(file_ext),
                       "r" = "📜", "rmd" = "📝", "css" = "🎨", "js" = "⚡",
                       "py" = "🐍", "json" = "📋", "txt" = "📄", "html" = "🌐",
                       "md" = "📖", "sh" = "⚙️", "yml" = "🔧", "xml" = "📰",
                       "📄")
        
        # Add line count for text files
        line_count <- ""
        if (tolower(file_ext) %in% c("r", "R", "rmd", "py", "js", "css", "txt", "md")) {
          tryCatch({
            lines <- length(readLines(file, warn = FALSE))
            line_count <- paste0(", ", lines, " lines")
          }, error = function(e) {})
        }
        
        writeLines(paste("  ", icon, file_name, paste0("(", size_str, line_count, ")")), con)
      }
    }
    
    writeLines("", con)
    writeLines("", con)
  }
  
  # ============================================================
  # SECTION 3: CODE FILES
  # ============================================================
  writeLines(paste0(rep("#", 80), collapse = ""), con)
  writeLines("#                          📄 CODE FILES                                        #", con)
  writeLines(paste0(rep("#", 80), collapse = ""), con)
  writeLines("", con)
  
  # Progress tracking
  total_files <- length(all_files)
  processed <- 0
  
  for (i in seq_along(all_files)) {
    file_path <- all_files[i]
    file_name <- basename(file_path)
    rel_path <- gsub(paste0("^", parent_folder, "/?"), "", file_path)
    file_ext <- tools::file_ext(file_name)
    file_size_mb <- file.info(file_path)$size / (1024^2)
    
    # Progress indicator
    processed <- processed + 1
    if (!quiet && processed %% 10 == 0) {
      cat(sprintf("\r📦 Processing: %d/%d files (%.1f%%)", processed, total_files, 
                  processed/total_files*100))
    }
    
    # File header
    writeLines("", con)
    writeLines(paste0(rep("#", 80), collapse = ""), con)
    writeLines(paste0("# FILE ", sprintf("%3d", i), " OF ", total_files), con)
    writeLines(paste0(rep("#", 80), collapse = ""), con)
    writeLines(paste("# Name:", file_name), con)
    writeLines(paste("# Path:", rel_path), con)
    writeLines(paste("# Type:", ifelse(file_ext == "", "unknown", toupper(file_ext))), con)
    writeLines(paste("# Size:", ifelse(file_size_mb < 0.001, 
                                       paste0(round(file_size_mb * 1024, 1), " KB"),
                                       paste0(round(file_size_mb, 2), " MB"))), con)
    
    # Add line count for text files
    if (tolower(file_ext) %in% c("r", "R", "rmd", "py", "js", "css", "txt", "md", "html", "json")) {
      tryCatch({
        lines <- readLines(file_path, warn = FALSE)
        writeLines(paste("# Lines:", length(lines)), con)
      }, error = function(e) {})
    }
    
    writeLines(paste0(rep("#", 80), collapse = ""), con)
    writeLines("", con)
    
    # Read and write file content
    file_content <- tryCatch({
      readLines(file_path, warn = FALSE, encoding = "UTF-8")
    }, error = function(e) {
      c(paste("# ERROR: Could not read file -", e$message))
    })
    
    writeLines(file_content, con)
    writeLines("", con)
  }
  
  # Close connection
  close(con)
  
  if (!quiet) cat("\n")
  
  # Print summary
  cat("\n", paste(rep("=", 80), collapse = ""), "\n")
  cat("✅ COMBINATION COMPLETE!\n")
  cat(paste(rep("=", 80), collapse = ""), "\n")
  cat("📄 Output file:", output_file, "\n")
  cat("📊 Total files combined:", length(all_files), "\n")
  cat("📁 File types:", paste(selected_extensions, collapse = ", "), "\n")
  cat("💾 Output size:", round(file.info(output_file)$size / (1024^2), 2), "MB\n")
  
  # Show breakdown by file type
  cat("\n📊 BREAKDOWN BY FILE TYPE:\n")
  ext_counts <- table(tools::file_ext(all_files))
  ext_counts <- sort(ext_counts, decreasing = TRUE)
  for (ext in names(ext_counts)) {
    if (ext == "") next
    cat(sprintf("   .%-10s: %3d files\n", ext, ext_counts[ext]))
  }
  
  # Show largest files
  cat("\n📊 LARGEST FILES (by lines):\n")
  file_stats <- data.frame(
    file = basename(all_files),
    lines = sapply(all_files, function(f) {
      tryCatch(length(readLines(f, warn = FALSE)), error = function(e) 0)
    }),
    stringsAsFactors = FALSE
  )
  file_stats <- file_stats[order(-file_stats$lines), ]
  head_files <- head(file_stats[file_stats$lines > 0, ], 10)
  for (i in 1:nrow(head_files)) {
    cat(sprintf("   %2d. %-40s (%5d lines)\n", i, head_files$file[i], head_files$lines[i]))
  }
  
  return(invisible(list(
    output_file = output_file,
    total_files = length(all_files),
    file_types = selected_extensions,
    files = all_files,
    file_stats = file_stats
  )))
}

write_folder_architecture <- function(folder_path, output_file = "folder_architecture.txt", 
                                      exclude_patterns = c("\\.rds$", "\\.RData$", "\\.Rhistory$", 
                                                           "^\\.", "~$", "\\.bak$"),
                                      max_depth = NULL, show_file_size = TRUE) {
  
  # Check if folder exists
  if (!dir.exists(folder_path)) {
    stop("Folder does not exist: ", folder_path)
  }
  
  # Normalize path
  folder_path <- normalizePath(folder_path, winslash = "/")
  
  # Open connection
  con <- file(output_file, "w", encoding = "UTF-8")
  
  # Write header
  writeLines(paste0(rep("=", 80), collapse = ""), con)
  writeLines("📁 FOLDER ARCHITECTURE REPORT", con)
  writeLines(paste0(rep("=", 80), collapse = ""), con)
  writeLines(paste0("Generated: ", format(Sys.time(), "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")), con)
  writeLines(paste0("Root Path: ", folder_path), con)
  writeLines("", con)
  
  # Count files
  all_files <- list.files(folder_path, recursive = TRUE, full.names = TRUE)
  writeLines(paste0("📊 Total files found: ", length(all_files)), con)
  writeLines("", con)
  
  # Function to check if file should be excluded
  should_exclude <- function(file_path) {
    basename_file <- basename(file_path)
    for (pattern in exclude_patterns) {
      if (grepl(pattern, basename_file, ignore.case = TRUE)) {
        return(TRUE)
      }
    }
    return(FALSE)
  }
  
  # Recursive function to print tree
  print_tree <- function(path, prefix = "", is_last = TRUE, depth = 0, con) {
    
    # Check depth limit
    if (!is.null(max_depth) && depth > max_depth) {
      return()
    }
    
    # Get base name
    base_name <- basename(path)
    
    # Determine connector
    connector <- if (is_last) "└── " else "├── "
    
    # Get file/dir info
    if (dir.exists(path)) {
      # Directory
      writeLines(paste0(prefix, connector, "📁 ", base_name, "/"), con)
      
      # Get children
      items <- list.files(path, full.names = TRUE, all.files = FALSE, no.. = TRUE)
      
      # Separate dirs and files
      dirs <- items[dir.exists(items)]
      files <- items[!dir.exists(items)]
      
      # Sort
      dirs <- sort(dirs)
      files <- sort(files)
      
      # Filter files
      files <- files[!sapply(files, should_exclude)]
      
      # Combine
      children <- c(dirs, files)
      
      # Update prefix
      new_prefix <- paste0(prefix, if (is_last) "    " else "│   ")
      
      # Process children
      for (i in seq_along(children)) {
        is_last_child <- (i == length(children))
        print_tree(children[i], new_prefix, is_last_child, depth + 1, con)
      }
      
    } else {
      # File - show with icon based on extension
      ext <- tools::file_ext(base_name)
      icon <- switch(tolower(ext),
                     "r" = "📜",
                     "rmd" = "📝",
                     "rds" = "💾",
                     "csv" = "📊",
                     "json" = "🔧",
                     "txt" = "📄",
                     "css" = "🎨",
                     "html" = "🌐",
                     "png" = "🖼️",
                     "jpg" = "🖼️",
                     "jpeg" = "🖼️",
                     "pdf" = "📕",
                     "md" = "📋",
                     "py" = "🐍",
                     "sh" = "⚙️",
                     "📄"
      )
      
      # Get file size if requested
      size_info <- ""
      if (show_file_size) {
        file_size <- file.info(path)$size
        if (!is.na(file_size)) {
          if (file_size < 1024) {
            size_info <- paste0(" (", file_size, " B)")
          } else if (file_size < 1024^2) {
            size_info <- paste0(" (", round(file_size/1024, 1), " KB)")
          } else {
            size_info <- paste0(" (", round(file_size/1024^2, 1), " MB)")
          }
        }
      }
      
      writeLines(paste0(prefix, connector, icon, " ", base_name, size_info), con)
    }
  }
  
  # Print tree starting from root
  writeLines("", con)
  writeLines("📂 DIRECTORY STRUCTURE:", con)
  writeLines(paste0("📁 ", basename(folder_path), "/"), con)
  
  # Get root children
  root_items <- list.files(folder_path, full.names = TRUE, all.files = FALSE, no.. = TRUE)
  root_dirs <- root_items[dir.exists(root_items)]
  root_files <- root_items[!dir.exists(root_items)]
  
  # Sort
  root_dirs <- sort(root_dirs)
  root_files <- sort(root_files)
  
  # Filter files
  root_files <- root_files[!sapply(root_files, should_exclude)]
  
  # Combine
  root_children <- c(root_dirs, root_files)
  
  # Process root children
  for (i in seq_along(root_children)) {
    is_last_child <- (i == length(root_children))
    print_tree(root_children[i], "", is_last_child, 1, con)
  }
  
  # Write file summary by extension
  writeLines("", con)
  writeLines(paste0(rep("=", 80), collapse = ""), con)
  writeLines("📊 FILE SUMMARY BY TYPE:", con)
  writeLines(paste0(rep("=", 80), collapse = ""), con)
  
  # Get all files (excluding excluded patterns)
  all_files_filtered <- all_files[!sapply(all_files, should_exclude)]
  
  # Count by extension
  ext_counts <- table(tools::file_ext(all_files_filtered))
  ext_counts <- sort(ext_counts, decreasing = TRUE)
  
  for (ext in names(ext_counts)) {
    ext_display <- ifelse(ext == "", "no extension", toupper(ext))
    writeLines(sprintf("  %-15s: %d files", ext_display, ext_counts[ext]), con)
  }
  
  # Write file size summary
  writeLines("", con)
  writeLines(paste0(rep("=", 80), collapse = ""), con)
  writeLines("📊 FILE SIZE SUMMARY:", con)
  writeLines(paste0(rep("=", 80), collapse = ""), con)
  
  total_size <- sum(file.info(all_files_filtered)$size, na.rm = TRUE)
  if (total_size < 1024) {
    total_size_display <- paste0(total_size, " B")
  } else if (total_size < 1024^2) {
    total_size_display <- paste0(round(total_size/1024, 2), " KB")
  } else if (total_size < 1024^3) {
    total_size_display <- paste0(round(total_size/1024^2, 2), " MB")
  } else {
    total_size_display <- paste0(round(total_size/1024^3, 2), " GB")
  }
  
  writeLines(paste0("  Total size: ", total_size_display), con)
  
  # Largest files
  if (length(all_files_filtered) > 0) {
    file_sizes <- file.info(all_files_filtered)$size
    largest_idx <- order(file_sizes, decreasing = TRUE)[1:min(10, length(all_files_filtered))]
    
    writeLines("", con)
    writeLines("📊 LARGEST FILES:", con)
    for (idx in largest_idx) {
      size <- file_sizes[idx]
      if (size < 1024) {
        size_display <- paste0(size, " B")
      } else if (size < 1024^2) {
        size_display <- paste0(round(size/1024, 1), " KB")
      } else {
        size_display <- paste0(round(size/1024^2, 1), " MB")
      }
      writeLines(sprintf("  %-50s %s", basename(all_files_filtered[idx]), size_display), con)
    }
  }
  
  # Close connection
  close(con)
  
  cat("\n✅ Architecture written to:", output_file, "\n")
  cat("📊 Total files:", length(all_files_filtered), "\n")
  cat("📁 Total directories:", length(list.dirs(folder_path, recursive = FALSE)), "\n")
  
  return(invisible(output_file))
}

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# SERVER
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
server <- function(input, output, session) {
  
  # --- Fixed username (used for reports) ---
  username <- toupper(Sys.info()[["user"]])
  
  # --- Session-only cart ---
  cart <- reactiveVal(list())
  
  # --- Active page: start on Home ---
  active_page <- reactiveVal("home")
  
  # --- Navigation observers (sidebar clicks) ---
  observeEvent(input$sidebar, {
    active_page(input$sidebar)
  })
  
  # --- Header cart button click -> same navigation as the old sidebar item ---
  observeEvent(input$header_cart_btn, {
    active_page("cart")
  })
  
  # --- Sync sidebar highlight with active_page (Problem 10) ---
  observeEvent(active_page(), {
    updateTabItems(session, "sidebar", selected = active_page())
  }, ignoreInit = FALSE)
  
  # --- Header cart icon (icon-only, live reactive count badge) ---
  output$header_cart_ui <- renderUI({
    n <- length(cart())
    actionButton(
      "header_cart_btn",
      label = tagList(
        icon("shopping-cart"),
        if (n > 0) tags$span(class = "header-cart-badge", n)
      ),
      class = "header-cart-btn",
      title = "Cart"
    )
  })
  
  # --- Sidebar menu (Cart added back, Problem 9) ---
  output$sidebar_menu <- renderMenu({
    sidebarMenu(
      id = "sidebar",
      menuItem("Home",               tabName = "home",          icon = icon("house")),
      menuItem("Input File Creation", tabName = "input_prep",   icon = icon("location-arrow")),
      menuItem("Result Extraction",   tabName = "result_extract", icon = icon("database")),
      menuItem("Secondary Modifier", tabName = "secmod",        icon = icon("shield-alt")),
      menuItem("Cart",               tabName = "cart",          icon = icon("shopping-cart"))  # <-- ADDED
    )
  })
  
  # --- Module servers ---
  home_nav <- callModule(homeServer, "home", cart = cart)
  callModule(inputPrepServer, "prep")
  callModule(resultExtractServer, "extract")
  callModule(secmod_server, "secmod", cart = cart, username = username)
  callModule(cart_server, "cart", cart = cart, username = username)
  
  # --- Home page cards/buttons drive navigation via a returned reactiveVal ---
  observeEvent(home_nav(), {
    if (!is.null(home_nav())) active_page(home_nav())
  })
  
  # --- Main UI (only the page body, navbar is now in dashboardHeader/Sidebar) ---
  output$app_ui <- renderUI({
    switch(
      active_page(),
      "home"             = homeUI("home"),
      "input_prep"       = inputPrepUI("prep"),
      "result_extract"   = resultExtractUI("extract"),
      "secmod"           = secmod_ui("secmod"),
      "cart"             = cart_ui("cart"),
      homeUI("home")         # fallback
    )
  })
}

shinyApp(ui, server)

## =============================================================================
## CART UTILITIES MODULE  (module/cart_utils.R)
## =============================================================================
## Handles persistence of each user's cart to cart/<username>_cart.rds and
## generates unique ids for cart items.
##
## Used by: App.R, vulnerability_server.R, secmod_server.R, cart_server.R
##
## Cart item structure (each element of the saved list):
##   list(
##     id         = "<unique id>",
##     module     = "Vulnerability" | "Secondary Modifier",
##     plot       = <ggplot object>,      # optional but usually present
##     commentary = "<character string>", # optional
##     timestamp  = <POSIXct>
##   )
## =============================================================================

CART_DIR <- "cart"

## -----------------------------------------------------------------------
## Build (and ensure) the path to a user's cart .rds file
## -----------------------------------------------------------------------
get_cart_path <- function(username) {

  if (!dir.exists(CART_DIR)) {
    dir.create(CART_DIR, recursive = TRUE)
  }

  safe_username <- gsub("[^A-Za-z0-9_-]", "_", as.character(username))

  file.path(CART_DIR, paste0(safe_username, "_cart.rds"))
}

## -----------------------------------------------------------------------
## Load a user's cart. Returns an empty list if none exists yet.
## -----------------------------------------------------------------------
load_cart <- function(username) {

  path <- get_cart_path(username)

  if (file.exists(path)) {
    tryCatch(
      readRDS(path),
      error = function(e) list()
    )
  } else {
    list()
  }
}

## -----------------------------------------------------------------------
## Save a user's cart back to disk
## -----------------------------------------------------------------------
save_cart <- function(username, cart_items) {

  path <- get_cart_path(username)

  saveRDS(cart_items, path)

  invisible(path)
}

## -----------------------------------------------------------------------
## Generate a unique id for a new cart item (timestamp + random suffix)
## -----------------------------------------------------------------------
generate_item_id <- function() {

  paste0(
    format(Sys.time(), "%Y%m%d%H%M%OS3"),
    "_",
    sample(1000:9999, 1)
  )
}
